HUTCN(
  (fea_conv): Conv2d(3, 80, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (post_unet_esa): ESA(
    (conv1): Conv2d(80, 20, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    (conv2): Conv2d(20, 20, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
    (conv3): Conv2d(20, 20, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
    (conv_f): Conv2d(20, 20, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    (conv4): Conv2d(20, 80, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
    (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
    (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
  )
  (post_unet_conv): Conv2d(80, 80, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (B1): P_HTCB(
    (tesa_in): TESA(
      (esa1): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(80, 20, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))

        (conv3): Conv2d(20, 20, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv_f): Conv2d(20, 20, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(20, 80, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa2): ESA(

        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa3): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(80, 20, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(20, 20, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(20, 20, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv_f): Conv2d(20, 20, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(20, 80, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
    )
    (tcn1): TCN(
      (conv3): Conv2d(80, 80, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
      (swinT): SwinT(
        (transformer_body): Sequential(
          (0): BasicLayer(
            (blocks): ModuleList(
              (0-2): 3 x SwinTransformerBlock(
                (norm1): LayerNorm((80,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
                (attn): WindowAttention(
                  (qkv): Linear(in_features=80, out_features=240, bias=True)
                  (proj): Linear(in_features=80, out_features=80, bias=True)
                  (softmax): Softmax(dim=-1)
                )
                (norm2): LayerNorm((80,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
                (mlp): Mlp(
                  (fc1): Linear(in_features=80, out_features=180, bias=True)
                  (act): GELU(approximate='none')
                  (fc2): Linear(in_features=180, out_features=80, bias=True)
                )
              )
            )
            (patch_embed): PatchEmbed(
              (norm): LayerNorm((80,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            )
            (patch_unembed): PatchUnEmbed()
          )
        )
      )
    )
    (tcn2): TCN(

    (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
  )
  (post_unet_conv): Conv2d(80, 80, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (B1): P_HTCB(
    (tesa_in): TESA(
      (esa1): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(80, 20, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(20, 20, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(20, 20, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv_f): Conv2d(20, 20, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))

        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )

        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
    )

            (blocks): ModuleList(
